3132026-11-11Navigation fix, no content change. A link to a late card (for example E133) or a long jump in the contents bar stopped short, around E9 or some fifteen cards on. Two causes: the contents bar followed the reader with scrollIntoView, which cancels a page scroll still under way as soon as the active entry leaves the bar's visible part; and the 205 figures carried no size, so the page grew as they loaded and the target moved. The bar now moves by its own scroll position, the build writes each figure's pixel size into the page, and smooth scrolling is off. Not checked in a browser.
